- 2026-10-10T08:33 `pr-body` M3jdk21#678: A text whose candidates verify long and often hands the rest of the scan to the reverse KMP of the plan (`reverseSkipSearch`): once eight candidates (`DENSE_CANDIDATES`) have verified as many units as were walked, the KMP continues below the failed candidate with nothing matched, every match ending above it ruled out already, so a degenerate text (a single-unit text against a needle ending in that unit) never costs more than the KMP did.
- 2026-10-10T08:33 `pr-body` M3jdk21#678: Random text never fires the hand-off.
- 2026-10-10T08:33 `pr-body` M3jdk21#678: The source is read raw, as the stock loops read it: the arguments fix every index inside the source (the highest start is at most the source length less the needle length; a verify never reads below the candidate's start).
